[ASSISTANT]
I'll remove resets from the input-skew and output-de-skew registers too (keeping only the valid pipeline reset, which zero-enables the output during the flush). Verilator initializes registers to 0, and the first c_valid is gated for the full latency, so this should be functionally safe and further cuts area.


  -> create_file(filename='design_p.sv', content="// Carry-save systolic array. All datapath registers (PE partial sums/\n// carry/activation/weight, input skew, output de-skew) omit reset; only\n// the c_valid pipeline retains reset so outputs are not checked until the\n// array has been flushed by zero injection.\n\nmodule tpu_pe\n  #(parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\t  clk,\n    input\t\t  rst,\n    input\t\t  ld,\n    input\t\t  en,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  w_north,\n    input [DW-1:0]\t  a_west,\n    input [ACCW-1:0]\t  s_north,\n    input [ACCW-1:0]\t  c_north,\n    output [DW-1:0]\t  w_south,\n    output reg [DW-1:0]\t  a_east,\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] s_south,\n    output reg [ACCW-1:0] c_south\n    );\n\n   reg [DW-1:0] w;\n   assign w_south = w;\n\n   wire [2*DW-1:0] prod = $signed(a_west) * $signed(w);\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] prod_ext = {{(ACCW-2*DW){prod[2*DW-1]}}, prod};\n\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] s_ab = s_north ^ c_north;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] sum_w = s_ab ^ prod_ext;\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] carry_w = {(s_north & c_north) | (s_ab & prod_ext), 1'b0};\n\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (ld)\n        w <= w_north;\n      if (en) begin\n         a_east <= a_west;\n         s_south <= sum_w;\n         c_south <= carry_w[ACCW-1:0];\n      end\n   end\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu_array\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tld,\n    input\t\ten,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_top,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_left,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_bot\n    );\n\n   wire [DW-1:0]   a_h [0:N-1][0:N];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] s_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [ACCW-1:0] c_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n   wire [DW-1:0]   w_v [0:N][0:N-1];\n\n   genvar\t   i, j;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_west\n         assign a_h[i][0] = a_left[i*DW +: DW];\n      end\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_edge\n         assign s_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign c_v[0][j] = {ACCW{1'b0}};\n         assign w_v[0][j] = w_top[j*DW +: DW];\n         assign c_bot[j*ACCW +: ACCW] = s_v[N][j] + c_v[N][j];\n      end\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_pe_row\n         for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_pe_col\n            tpu_pe #(.DW(DW), .ACCW(ACCW)) u_pe\n              (.clk        (clk),\n               .rst        (rst),\n               .ld         (ld),\n               .en         (en),\n               .w_north    (w_v[i][j]),\n               .a_west     (a_h[i][j]),\n               .s_north    (s_v[i][j]),\n               .c_north    (c_v[i][j]),\n               .w_south    (w_v[i+1][j]),\n               .a_east     (a_h[i][j+1]),\n               .s_south    (s_v[i+1][j]),\n               .c_south    (c_v[i+1][j]));\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule tpu\n  #(parameter N = 8,\n    parameter DW = 8,\n    parameter ACCW = 32,\n    parameter IACCW = 19)\n   (\n    input\t\tclk,\n    input\t\trst,\n    input\t\tw_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\tw_data,\n    input\t\ta_valid,\n    input [N*DW-1:0]\ta_data,\n    output\t\tc_valid,\n    output [N*ACCW-1:0]\tc_data\n    );\n\n   localparam LAT = 2*N - 1;\n   wire\t      ld = w_valid;\n   wire\t      en = ~w_valid;\n\n   wire [N*DW-1:0] a_left;\n   genvar\t   i;\n   generate\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i = i + 1) begin : g_skew\n         wire [DW-1:0] din =\n\t\t       a_valid ? a_data[i*DW +: DW] : {DW{1'b0}};\n         if (i == 0) begin : g_skew0\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = din;\n         end\n         else begin : g_skewn\n            reg [DW-1:0] sr [0:i-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  sr[0] <= din;\n                  for (k = 1; k < i; k = k + 1)\n                    sr[k] <= sr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign a_left[i*DW +: DW] = sr[i-1];\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   wire [N*IACCW-1:0] c_bot;\n   tpu_array #(.N(N), .DW(DW), .ACCW(IACCW)) u_array\n     (.clk(clk), .rst(rst), .ld(ld), .en(en),\n      .w_top(w_data), .a_left(a_left), .c_bot(c_bot));\n\n   wire [N*ACCW-1:0] c_before_ext;\n   genvar j;\n   generate\n      for (j = 0; j < N; j = j + 1) begin : g_deskew\n         localparam D = N - 1 - j;\n         wire [IACCW-1:0] cin = c_bot[j*IACCW +: IACCW];\n         if (D == 0) begin : g_deskew0\n            assign c_before_ext[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                 {{(ACCW-IACCW){cin[IACCW-1]}}, cin};\n         end\n         else begin : g_deskewn\n            reg [IACCW-1:0] dr [0:D-1];\n            integer k;\n            always @(posedge clk) begin\n               if (en) begin\n                  dr[0] <= cin;\n                  for (k = 1; k < D; k = k + 1)\n                    dr[k] <= dr[k-1];\n               end\n            end\n            assign c_before_ext[j*ACCW +: ACCW] =\n                 {{(ACCW-IACCW){dr[D-1][IACCW-1]}}, dr[D-1]};\n         end\n      end\n   endgenerate\n\n   assign c_data = c_before_ext;\n\n   reg [LAT-1:0] vpipe;\n   always @(posedge clk) begin\n      if (rst)\n        vpipe <= {LAT{1'b0}};\n      else if (en)\n        vpipe <= {vpipe[LAT-2:0], a_valid};\n   end\n   assign c_valid = vpipe[LAT-1];\n\nendmodule")

The design session is now over. Final result: PASS, best cost: 2.782e+06 area_delay_product.

Please write a brief summary covering:
1. What approaches did you try and what worked best?
2. What optimizations had the most impact?
3. What didn't work or caused regressions?
4. Lessons learned and what you would do differently next time.

Your design files are carried over to the next agent, so refer to them by filename where useful.
Be concise and specific.

[ASSISTANT]
Summary:

1. **Approaches tried and best result**
   - Started from the provided `starting_point.v` (32-bit partial sums, per-PE carry-propagate adds): **4.624e6 area-delay product**.
   - Reduced internal accumulator width from 32 to 19 bits in `design_a.sv` / `design_c.sv` while sign-extending only at output: ~**3.226e6**.
   - Replaced each PE’s carry-propagate accumulator with carry-save redundant accumulation (`s,c`) so the final propagate add happens once at the column bottom (`design_d.sv`): ~**2.869e6**.
   - Removed resets from the PE datapath registers while keeping the `c_valid` pipeline reset (`design_n2.sv`): **2.782e6**, the best design this session.
   - **Best file:** `design_n2.sv` — PASS, 0/6782 checks, area 4220, delay 659 ps, area-delay 2782014.9.

2. **Most impactful optimizations**
   - Narrowing the internal accumulator from 32 to 19 bits. The true signed 8-term result needs 19 bits, and this removed a large fraction of flip-flop/adder logic.
   - Carry-save accumulation in the PE array. Moving the carry-propagate addition out of each PE reduced the systolic register-to-register logic substantially.
   - Removing reset from the PE partial-sum/carry/weight/activation datapath registers. The cycle-accurate behavior is still correct because zeros flush the pipe before the first checked result; keeping reset only on the `c_valid` pipeline preserves simulation/check correctness.

3. **What didn’t work or regressed**
   - **18-bit internal accumulator** (`design_b.sv`, `design_e.sv`) failed. The extreme corner `8 * (-128 * -128) = 131072` requires signed 19 bits.
   - **Removing all resets including `vpipe`** (`design_n.sv`) failed; early `c_valid` assertions were checked before the flush was complete.
   - **Combinational activation broadcast with uniform output delay** (`design_g.sv`) failed functionally; the systolic timing/skew must remain per-row/per-column.
   - **Per-row adaptive accumulator widths** (`design_h/h2/j`) failed, and one version also had a synthesis syntax error.
   - **Moving the final propagate add after de-skew** (`design_k.sv`) passed but increased area substantially, producing a worse area-delay product (~3.072e6).
   - Removing even more reset logic in `design_p.sv` was slightly worse than `design_n2.sv` (2.802e6 vs 2.782e6), so the chosen reset structure matters.

4. **Lessons learned / next steps**
   - The biggest area savings come from proving exact accumulator bounds and using redundant carry-save form, not just tweaking synthesis constraints.
   - Reset removal is very effective but must be applied carefully: keep resets on the control/valid path that masks the output.
   - `target_delay` sweeps did not improve the best cost; default 500 ps was already best. The structural RTL changes mattered much more.
   - If continuing, I would explore:
     - Booth or other multiplier structures per PE;
     - retiming the carry-save tree inside/around PEs;
     - partial final addition decomposition across columns;
     - cleanup of currently unused upper accumulator slices if safe for row-dependent widths.
